} // Check if the text contains spaces, hyphens, or '(' const hasSpaces = text.includes(' '); const hasHyphen = text.includes('-'); const hasParentheses = text.includes('('); const hasSlash = text.includes('/'); if (hasSpaces || hasHyphen || hasParentheses || hasSlash) { // Split the text into parts by spaces and separators const regex = /([\s\-()/]+)/; const parts = text.split(regex); // Process and capitalize words const capitalizedParts = parts.map(part => { if (part.match(/[\s\-()]+/)) { // Retain spaces and separators return part; } else { const lowercasedPart = part.toLowerCase(); if (lowercasedPart.startsWith('mc') && lowercasedPart.length > 2) { // Handle the 'McDonalds' case where the third letter needs to be capitalized return 'Mc' + part.charAt(2).toUpperCase() + lowercasedPart.substring(3); } else { // Check if the part is in a whitelist before transforming return uppercaseWhitelist.includes(part) ? part : whitelist.includes(lowercasedPart) ? lowercasedPart : lowercasedPart.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + lowercasedPart.slice(1); } } }); // Join the processed parts back together return capitalizedParts.join(''); } else { // If there's only one word, capitalize the first letter unless it's in the uppercase whitelist return uppercaseWhitelist.includes(text) ? text : text.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + text.slice(1).toLowerCase(); } } Related Information0ef383534783b47d7bb94c668f2f7d91d551d8bab69e4106b7e19e6197786f9dNOMINATION_SOURCE; SENATE_ACTION_IF_REQUIRED; COMMISSION_OR_APPOINTMENT_INSTRUMENT; OATH_OR_ENTRY_EVIDENCE; TERM_AND_VACANCY_STATUSEXCF-000874Appointment-source context does not prove nomination, confirmation, commission, oath, entry on duty, tenure, or current service.NO_SPECIAL_CONFLICT_RULE;
